# Cleanroom Relicensing

Cleanroom has relicensed the repository as of **2026-09-29 11:28:08 UTC**.
It was not done on a whim and had been discussed among multiple Cleanroom team members for a while.
The goal is solely to prevent rebrands while keeping the project transparent as always.

Nevertheless, we have overlooked its impact, misconceptions, and consent from previous contributors.

Some people might consider contributor consent during the migration to be the major flaw of the new CleanroomMC License.
This will be addressed in the second part of the RELICENSING file. For now,
no candidate is recorded as having refused or granted permission. Consent remains unverified.

## Relicensing Motivation

As you all know, rebranding, AI code laundering, and porting are enduring phenomena among 1.12 communities.
And we feel the need to relicense mainly the `module/cleanroom` part under a closed-source but source-visible license to protect our code from rebrands,
as we have been adding more and more standalone features and APIs since 0.7-alpha.

It is understandable that people may find the LGPL-to-ARR-ish migration hard to swallow. Be it blood; be it tears; it is part of the roadmap.

## Impact on Players, Modders, Modpackers, and Future Contributors

This migration has no actual impact on most of the audience.

For players and modders, you are still allowed to
download, run, play the game, or investigate the source code, and even share a modified clone privately.
Modders are allowed to apply Mixins, ASM transformations, and Java agents to Cleanroom code, and
use any Cleanroom API without affecting your mod's license.

For modpackers, you are still allowed to distribute your modpack via Cleanroom Relauncher as usual.
The new license will not affect your pack's license either.

For future contributors, you are still allowed to fork Cleanroom on GitHub and submit PRs, and your contributions
will be governed by the license applicable to the files or components you contribute to. See the Mixed Licensing section for details.

## Mixed Licensing

The new CleanroomMC License allows mixed licensing,
and `module/forge/`, `module/minecraft/patches/`, `module/builtin/configanytime/`, `module/builtin/mica/`, `module/builtin/mixinbooter/`, and `module/kirino/`
are all excluded and remain unaffected.

The consent issue is concentrated in `module/cleanroom/`, and we are actively trying to address it.

Some files and code snippets have been moved from Forge's `module/forge/` to Cleanroom's `module/cleanroom/`. They remain unaffected as well.

# Relicensing Audit

This part is a relicensing audit and the code snippets that require explicit contributor consent will be listed.

This audit was prepared with AI assistance since there are thousands of commits to review.

- Snapshot: [abae1d8258d8](https://github.com/CleanroomMC/Cleanroom/commit/abae1d8258d814fa1e54eba11a8a411942bc25bc) (`abae1d8258d814fa1e54eba11a8a411942bc25bc`)
- Audit reference time: October 17, 2026, 3:00 p.m. EDT (UTC−04:00)

## Scope

| Component                                                           | Treatment                                                           |
|---------------------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------|
| `module/cleanroom/`                                                 | Included: 156 files, with historical provenance checked separately. |
| Root files, `.github/`, build/configuration files, `.idea/icon.svg` | Included.                                                           |
| `module/forge/`                                                     | Excluded: LGPL-2.1.                                                 |
| `module/minecraft/patches/`                                         | Excluded.                                                           |
| `module/builtin/configanytime/` and `mica/`                         | Excluded: MIT.                                                      |
| `module/builtin/mixinbooter/`                                       | Excluded: LGPL-2.1.                                                 |
| `module/kirino`                                                     | Excluded: independent submodule.                                    |
| Gradle wrapper scripts and JAR                                      | Excluded: external Apache-2.0 material.                             |

Code moved from Forge or another separately licensed component into Cleanroom still needs its original provenance checked.

## Migration timeline

| Date                        | Commit                                                                                                   | What changed                                                                                                          |
|-----------------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| 2023-02-15                  | [f45360e5e02b](https://github.com/CleanroomMC/Cleanroom/commit/f45360e5e02bb8a407f0d7831b41d6f902769085) | Initial root LGPL-2.1 license.                                                                                        |
| 2026-09-23                  | [6bb602ec6f0c](https://github.com/CleanroomMC/Cleanroom/commit/6bb602ec6f0c8c92545818655b1cdd80aba9a31f) | Module overhaul. The root LICENSE moved unchanged to `module/forge/LICENSE`; no replacement root license existed yet. |
| **2026-09-29 11:28:08 UTC** | [6e9b4c16c5fc](https://github.com/CleanroomMC/Cleanroom/commit/6e9b4c16c5fceff5bbf34429ee0c00e062306956) | CleanroomMC License v1.0 added at the root, alongside bulk header/format changes.                                     |
| 2026-10-09                  | [a5292ca18931](https://github.com/CleanroomMC/Cleanroom/commit/a5292ca1893115805efc910193c074f8a04ca02d) | Mica MIT notice added; further bulk formatting/header changes.                                                        |
| 2026-10-17                  | [d47fa066ab2a](https://github.com/CleanroomMC/Cleanroom/commit/d47fa066ab2a1d094233fc42595b4fa8a757978c) | ConfigAnytime MIT and MixinBooter LGPL notices restored.                                                              |

The new license’s §§2.1–2.5 preserve more specific notices and reject automatic relicensing through movement or copying. §9 addresses new submissions; it does not supply missing permission for earlier contributions. §10 preserves separately licensed material.

### 3. Artwork, author tags and independent components

- Artwork: [PR #286](https://github.com/CleanroomMC/Cleanroom/pull/286) introduced `.idea/icon.svg`. [DC-040](#dc-040) survives under ordinary blame. HIKARIE/Oganesson897 is currently identified by that PR as Gebardensprache, GitHub ID 101081378; confirm rights in the underlying artwork.
- Author tags: [8d9280b93a44](https://github.com/CleanroomMC/Cleanroom/commit/8d9280b93a448a3a613261839a2cbb43bd7f0085) removed erroneous AI-added ZZZank tags. Source tags were not treated as ownership evidence.
- Patches: the 12 patch-origin proposals in the original catalog are filtered; their unchanged context, imports, rendering constants or field declarations do not establish authorship by the patch submitter. Minecraft patches remain excluded.
- Compute: historical `feature/compute` material has a separate EΣrie Source Available Licence.
